### Reading and Writing Tools / Text Editors

- [Aurora Editor](wiki/aurora-editor.md) · [upstream](<https://auroraeditor.com/>) — Lightweight Code Editor (IDE) for macOS. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/AuroraEditor/AuroraEditor)
- [Bootstrap Studio](wiki/bootstrap-studio.md) · [upstream](<https://bootstrapstudio.io/>) — A powerful desktop app for creating responsive websites using the Bootstrap framework.
- [Brackets](wiki/brackets.md) · [upstream](<https://brackets.io>) — A modern, open source text editor that understands web design. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/brackets-cont/brackets/) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [CodeEdit](wiki/codeedit.md) · [upstream](<https://www.codeedit.app/>) — A lightweight, natively-built editor. Open source. Free forever. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/CodeEditApp/CodeEdit) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [CotEditor](wiki/coteditor.md) · [upstream](<https://coteditor.com>) — Lightweight plain-text editor for macOS. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/coteditor/CotEditor/) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [Cursor](wiki/cursor.md) · [upstream](<https://cursor.com/>) — AI code editor with autocomplete, chat, and coding agent features. !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [Emacs](wiki/emacs.md) · [upstream](<https://www.emacswiki.org/emacs/EmacsForMacOS>) — Popular Unix-based text editor for programmers and system administrators. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://git.savannah.gnu.org/cgit/) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\] \[!\[Awesome List\]\[awesome-list Icon\]\](https://github.com/emacs-tw/awesome-emacs#readme)

- [Itsypad](wiki/itsypad.md) · [upstream](<https://itsypad.app>) — A tiny, fast scratchpad and clipboard manager for macOS. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/nickustinov/itsypad-macos) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [Lapce](wiki/lapce.md) · [upstream](<https://lapce.dev/>) — Lightning-fast and powerful code editor. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/lapce/lapce) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [LightTable](wiki/lighttable.md) · [upstream](<https://lighttable.com/>) — The next generation code editor. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/LightTable/LightTable) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [LiteEdit](wiki/liteedit.md) · [upstream](<https://arietan.github.io/lite-edit/>) — Fast, native macOS code editor under 1 MB — Swift and AppKit, no Electron. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/arietan/lite-edit) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [MacVim](wiki/macvim.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/macvim-dev/macvim>) — the text editor Vim - for macOS. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/macvim-dev/macvim) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [micro](wiki/micro.md) · [upstream](<https://micro-editor.github.io>) — Modern and intuitive terminal-based text editor. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/ory/editor) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]

- [NeatEditor](wiki/neateditor.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/NeatMacApps/NeatEditor>) — Fast, native macOS plain-text editor for distraction-free writing. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/NeatMacApps/NeatEditor) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\] !\[Native App\]\[Native Icon\]

- [Windsurf](wiki/windsurf.md) · [upstream](<https://windsurf.com/>) — AI code editor with agentic coding, autocomplete, and MCP support. !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]

- [Orca](wiki/orca.md) · [upstream](<https://onorca.dev>) — An open-source IDE for running parallel AI coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini, OpenCode), each in its own isolated git worktree. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/stablyai/orca) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]

### Reading and Writing Tools / Markdown Tools

- [Archimedes](wiki/archimedes.md) · [upstream](<https://furnacecreek.org/archimedes/>) — Native macOS Markdown editor geared toward mathematical writing with inline LaTeX support.
- [Edmund](wiki/edmund.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/I7T5/Edmund>) — Minimal, native macOS Markdown editor with live preview; works with your existing files, no vault. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/I7T5/Edmund) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\] !\[Native App\]\[Native Icon\]
- [EME](wiki/eme.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/egoist/eme>) — Open-source Markdown editor with an interface like Chrome. !\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]

- [Imark](wiki/imark.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/migsilva89/imark>) — Open-source Markdown reader that stores your comments inside the document itself as HTML comments. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/migsilva89/imark) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\] !\[Native App\]\[Native Icon\]
- [JustMD](wiki/justmd.md) · [upstream](<https://justmd.nuta.life/>) — Open-source Markdown editor for local files that hides syntax outside the line being edited. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/yuraist/just-md) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\] \[!\[App Store\]\[app-store Icon\]\](https://apps.apple.com/app/id6779422717)

- [Marked 2](wiki/marked-2.md) · [upstream](<https://marked2app.com/>) — This is the Markdown preview with an elegant and powerful set of tools for all writers.
- [MarkText](wiki/marktext.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/marktext/marktext>) — Next generation markdown editor, running on platforms of MacOS Windows and Linux. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/marktext/marktext) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [MarkViewer](wiki/markviewer.md) · [upstream](<https://markviewer.com>) — Markdown viewer and editor for macOS with AI-assisted editing. !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [Marp](wiki/marp.md) · [upstream](<https://marp.app>) — Markdown presentation writer with cross-platform support. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/marp-team/marp) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [Marxico](wiki/marxico.md) · [upstream](<https://marxi.co/>) — Delicate Markdown editor for Evernote. Reliable storage and sync.
- [MDViewer](wiki/mdviewer.md) · [upstream](<https://mmmikael.github.io/mdviewer/>) — Fast Markdown viewer with live reload, Quick Look previews, Mermaid, math and optional AI summaries with your own or a local model. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/mmmikael/mdviewer) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [MWeb](wiki/mweb.md) · [upstream](<https://www.mweb.im/>) — Pro Markdown writing, and static blog generator App.

- [Scratch](wiki/scratch.md) · [upstream](<https://www.ericli.io/scratch>) — Lightweight, offline-first markdown notes app with WYSIWYG editing and local AI agent support. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/erictli/scratch) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]
- [Splat](wiki/splat.md) · [upstream](<https://github.com/sventmueller/splat>) — Native macOS Markdown editor in Rust where both the source and the live preview are editable. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/sventmueller/splat) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\] !\[Native App\]\[Native Icon\]
- [Textus](wiki/textus.md) · [upstream](<https://textus.catvinci.com>) — WYSIWYG Markdown editor with math, tables, Mermaid and an optional AI writing assistant that works with cloud or local models. \[!\[Open-Source Software\]\[OSS Icon\]\](https://github.com/CatVinci-Studio/Textus) !\[Freeware\]\[Freeware Icon\]

## Update and validate

```sh
python -m pip install 'git+https://github.com/OpenNorms/openshelf.git'
python tools/check.py
openshelf lint .
python tools/ingest.py --date 2026-11-04
```

Default ingestion uses the pinned raw snapshot. To update, capture a reviewed upstream README and commit in a new dated directory with `capture.json`, retain its notices, update raw checksums, then run `python tools/ingest.py --source raw/YYYY-MM-DD --date YYYY-MM-DD`. Existing UUIDs and creation dates are preserved. Authored brief/full/deep bodies survive updates; removed resources are retained as historical records.
